- Originally we have 1024 time series data points
- VQ compresses it to 512 tokens
- BPE shrinks it down by a factor of four to around 
  128 tokens ^FPVQdj1M

Algorithm to generate BPE codebook:
1. Find the most common pair of tokens
2. Replace that pair with a new token
3. Repeat ^6hlLwUJj

Apply BPE, substituting common token groups with their equivalent BPE token ^Y1LELFju
